## 読み取り専用データの取得範囲

Ghidraが定義した境界から先頭32バイトまで取得。型は推定値です。ポインタ、再配置、書込可能領域は取得理由を別記します。

## 読み取り専用データ

- 参照命令 [`0090dc58`](#insn-0090dc58)

~~~json
{
  "source": "0090dc58",
  "target": "Stack[-0x60]",
  "target_space": "stack",
  "resolution": "boundary_unknown"
}
~~~
- 参照命令 [`0090dc5c`](#insn-0090dc5c)

~~~json
{
  "source": "0090dc5c",
  "target": "Stack[-0x50]",
  "target_space": "stack",
  "resolution": "boundary_unknown"
}
~~~
- 参照命令 [`0090dc60`](#insn-0090dc60)

~~~json
{
  "source": "0090dc60",
  "target": "Stack[-0x40]",
  "target_space": "stack",
  "resolution": "boundary_unknown"
}
~~~

  "source": "0090dc64",
  "target": "Stack[-0x30]",
  "target_space": "stack",
  "resolution": "boundary_unknown"
}
~~~
